Identify values that are far outside expected range\n        # These are likely in different units\n        far_outliers = df[(df[col] > max_val * 10) | (df[col] < min_val / 10)]\n\n        if len(far_outliers) > 0:\n            # Determine likely unit and apply appropriate conversion\n\n            # Creatinine: µmol/L to mg/dL (divide by 88.4, or multiply by 0.0113)\n            if 'Creatinine' in col and col != 'Urine_Creatinine':\n                # Values in µmol/L need to be converted to mg/dL\n                # µmol/L * 0.0113 = mg/dL\n                mask = (df[col] > max_val * 10) & (df[col] < max_val * 1000)\n                if mask.sum() > 0:\n                    df.loc[mask, col] = df.loc[mask, col] * 0.0113\n                    print(f\"Converted {mask.sum()} {col} values from µmol/L to mg/dL\")\n\n            # Phosphorus: mmol/L to mg/dL (multiply by 3.1)\n            elif col == 'Phosphorus':\n                mask = (df[col] > max_val * 5) & (df[col] < max_val * 50)\n                if mask.sum() > 0:\n                    df.loc[mask, col] = df.loc[mask, col] * 3.1\n                    print(f\"Converted {mask.sum()} {col} values from mmol/L to mg/dL\")\n\n            # Hemoglobin: g/L to g/dL (divide by 10, or multiply by 0.1)\n            elif col == 'Hemoglobin':\n                mask = (df[col] > max_val * 5) & (df[col] < max_val * 100)\n                if mask.sum() > 0:\n                    df.loc[mask, col] = df.loc[mask, col] * 0.1\n                    print(f\"Converted {mask.sum()} {col} values from g/L to g/dL\")\n\n            # Bilirubin: µmol/L to mg/dL (multiply by 0.0585)\n            elif 'Bilirubin' in col:\n                mask = (df[col] > max_val * 10) & (df[col] < max_val * 200)\n                if mask.sum() > 0:\n                    df.loc[mask, col] = df.loc[mask, col] * 0.0585\n                    print(f\"Converted {mask.sum()} {col} values from µmol/L to mg/dL\")\n\n            # Albumin and Total Protein: g/L to g/dL (multiply by 0.1)\n            elif col in ['Albumin_Serum', 'Total_Protein']:\n                mask = (df[col] > max_val * 5) & (df[col] < max_val * 200)\n                if mask.sum() > 0:\n                    df.loc[mask, col] = df.loc[mask, col] * 0.1\n                    print(f\"Converted {mask.sum()} {col} values from g/L to g/dL\")\n\n            # Cholesterol: mmol/L to mg/dL (multiply by 38.67)\n            elif 'Cholesterol' in col and col != 'LDL_Cholesterol':\n                mask = (df[col] > max_val * 5) & (df[col] < max_val * 50)\n                if mask.sum() > 0:\n                    df.loc[mask, col] = df.loc[mask, col] * 38.67\n                    print(f\"Converted {mask.sum()} {col} values from mmol/L to mg/dL\")\n\n            # LDL_Cholesterol: mmol/L to mg/dL\n            elif col == 'LDL_Cholesterol':\n                mask = (df[col] > max_val * 5) & (df[col] < max_val * 50)\n                if mask.sum() > 0:\n                    df.loc[mask, col] = df.loc[mask, col] * 38.67\n                    print(f\"Converted {mask.sum()} {col} values from mmol/L to mg/dL\")\n\n            # Triglycerides: mmol/L to mg/dL (multiply by 88.57)\n            elif col == 'Triglycerides':\n                mask = (df[col] > max_val * 2) & (df[col] < max_val * 50)\n                if mask.sum() > 0:\n                    df.loc[mask, col] = df.loc[mask, col] * 88.57\n                    print(f\"Converted {mask.sum()} {col} values from mmol/L to mg/dL\")\n\n            # Uric Acid: µmol/L to mg/dL (multiply by 0.0168)\n            elif col == 'Uric_Acid':\n                mask = (df[col] > max_val * 10) & (df[col] < max_val * 1000)\n                if mask.sum() > 0:\n                    df.loc[mask, col] = df.loc[mask, col] * 0.0168\n                    print(f\"Converted {mask.sum()} {col} values from µmol/L to mg/dL\")\n\n            # Lactate: mg/dL to mmol/L (multiply by 0.111)\n            elif col == 'Lactate':\n                mask = (df[col] > max_val * 10) & (df[col] < max_val * 100)\n                if mask.sum() > 0:\n                    df.loc[mask, col] = df.loc[mask, col] * 0.111\n                    print(f\"Converted {mask.sum()} {col} values from mg/dL to mmol/L\")\n\n            # Ferritin: µg/L to ng/mL (same value, just unit change)\n            elif col == 'Ferritin':\n                mask = (df[col] > max_val * 5) & (df[col] < max_val * 100)\n                if mask.sum() > 0:\n                    df.loc[mask, col] = df.loc[mask, col] * 1.0\n                    print(f\"Converted {mask.sum()} {col} values from µg/L to ng/mL\")\n\n    return df\n\ndef round_to_two_decimals(df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Round all numeric values to 2 decimal places.\n    \"\"\"\n    numeric_columns = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        # Round to 2 decimal places\n        df[col] = df[col].round(2)\n\n    return df\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"\n    Main function to harmonize lab data.\n    \"\"\"\n    print(\"Starting lab data harmonization...\")\n\n    # Applying unit harmonization based on physiological ranges...\")\n    df = apply_unit_conversions(df)\n\n    # Round to 2 decimal places\n    print(\"\\n5. Convert to string if not already\n    value_str = str(value).strip()\n\n    # Remove any quotes\n    value_str = value_str.strip('\"').strip(\"'\")\n\n    # Handle empty strings\n    if value_str == '' or value_str == ' ':\n        return np.nan\n\n    # Replace decimal commas with dots\n    # But be careful: if there are multiple commas, they might be thousand separators\n    # We'll assume that if there's a comma, it's a decimal separator\n    # and dots are thousand separators (European format)\n    if ',' in value_str and '.' in value_str:\n        # European format: \"1.234,56\" -> \"1234.56\"\n        value_str = value_str.replace('.', '').replace(',', '.')\n    elif ',' in value_str:\n        # Only comma present: \"12,34\" -> \"12.34\"\n        # But check if it looks like a decimal (one comma, 1-2 digits after)\n        comma_count = value_str.count(',')\n        if comma_count == 1:\n            parts = value_str.split(',')\n            if len(parts) == 2 and len(parts[1]) <= 2:\n                value_str = value_str.replace(',', '.')\n            else:\n                # Multiple digits after comma or multiple commas, treat as thousand separator\n                value_str = value_str.replace(',', '')\n        else:\n            # Multiple commas, treat all as thousand separators\n            value_str = value_str.replace(',', '')\n\n    # Remove any remaining spaces\n    value_str = value_str.replace(' ', '')\n\n    # Try to parse as float\n    try:\n        return float(value_str)\n    except (ValueError, TypeError):\n        return np.nan\n\ndef convert_scientific_notation(df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Convert scientific notation to standard numeric format.\n    \"\"\"\n    # For columns where we know specific conversion factors\n        if col in conversion_factors:\n            # Try each possible conversion\n            for conversion_name, factor in conversion_factors[col].items():\n                # Apply conversion to values that are out of range\n                if '_to_' in conversion_name:\n                    # Check if values are likely in the wrong unit\n                    # (very high values that would make sense with conversion)\n                    threshold_low = min_val / factor\n                    threshold_high = max_val / factor\n\n                    # Values that would be out of range without conversion\n                    # but in range with conversion\n                    mask = (\n                        (df[col] > max_val) &  # Above normal range\n                        (df[col] <= max_val * 10) &  # Try different conversion factors based on typical unit conversions\n            possible_conversions = []\n\n            if 'Creatinine' in col:\n                possible_conversions = [0.0113]  # µmol/L to mg/dL\n            elif 'Phosphorus' in col:\n                possible_conversions = [3.1]  # mmol/L to mg/dL\n            elif 'Hemoglobin' in col and df[col].max() < 50:  # Likely in g/L\n                possible_conversions = [0.1]  # g/L to g/dL\n            elif col in ['Total_Bilirubin', 'Direct_Bilirubin']:\n                possible_conversions = [0.0585]  # µmol/L to mg/dL\n            elif 'Cholesterol' in col:\n                possible_conversions = [38.67]  # mmol/L to mg/dL\n            elif col == 'Triglycerides':\n                possible_conversions = [88.57]  # mmol/L to mg/dL\n            elif col in ['Albumin_Serum', 'Total_Protein']:\n                possible_conversions = [0.1]  # g/L to g/dL\n            elif 'Uric_Acid' in col:\n                possible_conversions = [0.0168]  # µmol/L to mg/dL\n            elif col == 'Lactate':\n                possible_conversions = [0.111]  # mg/dL to mmol/L (reverse conversion)\n\n            for factor in possible_conversions:\n                # Find values that would be in range after conversion\n                converted_mask = (\n                    (df[col] * factor >= min_val) &\n                    (df[col] * factor <= max_val) &\n                    ((df[col] < min_val) | (df[col] > max_val))\n                )\n\n                if converted_mask.sum() > 0:\n                    df.loc[converted_mask, col] = df[col] * factor\n                    print(f\"Applied conversion factor {factor} to {converted_mask.sum()} values in {col}\")\n\n    return df\n\ndef round_to_two_decimals(df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Round all numeric values to 2 decimal places.\n    \"\"\"\n    numeric_columns = [col for col in df.columns if col != 'patient_id']\n\n    for col in numeric_columns:\n        # Convert to string if not already\n    value_str = str(value).strip()\n\n    # Remove any quotes\n    value_str = value_str.strip('\"').strip(\"'\")\n\n    # Handle empty strings\n    if value_str == '' or value_str == ' ':\n        return np.nan\n\n    # Handle scientific notation first (e.g., \"7.6160e+00\")\n    # These should be kept as-is for now\n\n    # Handle European decimal format: comma as decimal separator\n    # Pattern: digits, optional comma, optional decimal digits\n    if ',' in value_str and '.' in value_str:\n        # Both comma and dot present: European format \"1.234,56\"\n        # Remove dots (thousand separators) and replace comma with dot\n        value_str = value_str.replace('.', '').replace(',', '.')\n    elif ',' in value_str:\n        # Only comma present: could be \"12,34\" or \"12,345\"\n        comma_count = value_str.count(',')\n\n        if comma_count == 1:\n            parts = value_str.split(',')\n            # If it's a simple case like \"12,34\" or \"12,3\" (1-3 decimal places)\n            if len(parts) == 2 and len(parts[1]) <= 2:\n                # Likely decimal separator\n                value_str = value_str.replace(',', '.')\n            else:\n                # Likely thousand separator or malformed\n                # Keep only digits\n                value_str = value_str.replace(',', '')\n        else:\n            # Multiple commas, treat all as thousand separators\n            value_str = value_str.replace(',', '')\n\n    # Remove any remaining spaces\n    value_str = value_str.replace(' ', '')\n\n    # Try to parse as float\n    try:\n        return float(value_str)\n    except (ValueError, TypeError):\n        return np.nan\n\ndef convert_scientific_notation(df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Convert scientific notation to standard numeric format.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Convert to string if not already\n    value_str = str(value).strip()\n\n    # Remove any quotes\n    value_str = value_str.strip('\"').strip(\"'\")\n\n    # Handle empty strings\n    if value_str == '' or value_str == ' ':\n        return np.nan\n\n    # Handle scientific notation first (e.g., \"7.6160e+00\")\n    # These should be kept as-is for now\n\n    # Handle European decimal format: comma as decimal separator\n    # Pattern: digits, optional comma, optional decimal digits\n    if ',' in value_str and '.' in value_str:\n        # Both comma and dot present: European format \"1.234,56\"\n        # Remove dots (thousand separators) and replace comma with dot\n        value_str = value_str.replace('.', '').replace(',', '.')\n    elif ',' in value_str:\n        # Only comma present: could be \"12,34\" or \"12,345\"\n        comma_count = value_str.count(',')\n\n        if comma_count == 1:\n            parts = value_str.split(',')\n            # If it's a simple case like \"12,34\", \"12,3\", or \"50,025\" (European decimal)\n            # Treat comma as decimal separator if part after comma is 1-3 digits\n            if len(parts) == 2 and 1 <= len(parts[1]) <= 3:\n                # Likely decimal separator (European format)\n                value_str = value_str.replace(',', '.')\n            else:\n                # Likely thousand separator or malformed\n                # Keep only digits\n                value_str = value_str.replace(',', '')\n        else:\n            # Multiple commas, treat all as thousand separators\n            value_str = value_str.replace(',', '')\n\n    # Remove any remaining spaces\n    value_str = value_str.replace(' ', '')\n\n    # Try to parse as float\n    try:\n        return float(value_str)\n    except (ValueError, TypeError):\n        return np.nan\n\ndef convert_scientific_notation(df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Convert scientific notation to standard numeric format.\n    \"\"\"\n    #
